- 🧭 **`ISF-VALUE-WIDTH-EMIT.0/.1` (`2026-06-21`) — Done (measurement-first, read-only, docs-only — GO): own + measure the ISF value-literal width-alignment defect; the naive "truncate the value" fix is dishonest.** New `ISF-*-EMIT` emitter-fidelity tree, spun out of the `CORPUS-COVERAGE.2` sweep as "Lever A" and picked as the **RAM-light** PNT slice (the host is a 6.3 GB machine at ~16% free RAM where a Docling re-ingest — the other open thread — is unsafe per the owner's non-negotiable RAM ceiling). The ISF emitter copies a `(rule … (SIGNAL value))` value literal verbatim (`render_isf_control_expression`, `ir/isf_ir.rs:1493-1495`) and never reconciles its width vs the signal's declared `(width N)`; FSMGen strict `--check` (`OperandContractValidationSupport.pm`) rejects a literal whose **notation width** (digit count — `0x7D`=8, `0b00`=2, **not** value) ≠ the LHS width, requiring an "explicit width-aligned source expression". **Measured (86 `.isf` + intent_ir + real `subs/fsmgen` Perl probes — RAM-safe, no cargo build):** 4 docs / 13 clauses (DTI ATST ×3, AXI+ACE ARTAGOP/BTAGMATCH ×6, AXI-gold AWCMO ×1, trace-bus ATID ×3); **DTI + TRACE confirmed FAIL** on OperandContract (*"RHS '2'b1' … incompatible width 2 for LHS width 1"*); the AXI docs fail FIRST on the orthogonal `(port expr)` grammar (masked). **Key finding:** all four signals are emitted at `(width 1)` **even when the IntentIR grounds a wider width** (ATID 7 / ARTAGOP 2) — the first-seen dedup (`isf_ir.rs:696-700`) skips the later concrete-width record and `.2a.i` only checks `actor_ports` → the over-width literal is a SYMPTOM, and ATID `0x7D`=125 is genuine, so truncating would fabricate. **GO fix design (`.2`, probe-validated):** recover the grounded width across ALL `signal_records`+`actor_ports`, then re-render the literal width-aligned `W'<radix><digits>` when `value<2^W`, else **residualize** — never truncate (ADR-0006 numeric-only). **Spun out:** DTI ATST upstream mis-attribution (`0b01` belongs to `ATTR_OVR.SHCFG`) + AXI `(port expr)`. Wire-gold orthogonal (only AXI `ihi0022_l` AWCMO, already failing on `(port expr)`; WIRE-BASED-100 measures extraction F1 not `.isf` bytes). **Gates:** docs-only — memory-arch + knowledge-map derive-and-diff GREEN (110 facts/772 keys); no Rust/CI/`.isf` change. Report `docs/research/isf-value-width-alignment-measurement.md`; KM `isf-value-width-operand-contract`. **`.2` is compile-gated → HELD until the host has RAM headroom.** **Status snapshot CHANGED:** new `active` tree `ISF-VALUE-WIDTH-EMIT` (`.0`/`.1` Done, GO); it is the current PNT build frontier. SpecForge NOT complete — substantive emitter-fidelity (bar #6) gap owned + designed.

- ✅ **`MEMORY-BOUNDED-INGEST.4c` (`2026-06-14`) — Done: size each page-range batch to the host's total RAM so a small machine completes instead of being RAM-guard-aborted.** `.1` batches at a fixed 64 pages — too large for a small machine (a 64-page batch + the Docling models crosses the danger ceiling → the `.4a` guard kills the run every time). `.4c` makes the batch adaptive to the machine: a constrained host COMPLETES (slower, smaller batches), speed flexes / quality invariant. **Signal = TOTAL physical RAM, NOT free memory** — total RAM is a per-machine constant so the batch (and the run's output) stays DETERMINISTIC per-machine (free memory jitters → non-deterministic boundaries; KM `evidence-build-nondeterminism`); transient pressure stays the `.4a` guard's job. `SPECFORGE_INGEST_BATCH_PAGES` is now a CEILING (default 64) that adaptive sizing only LOWERS via a jitter-free RAM ladder (`>= 16 GB` → ceiling, `8–16 GB` → ≤32, `4–8 GB` → ≤16, `< 4 GB` → floor 8; target ~30 % of RAM per 64-page-batch peak from the `.2` CHI datum). Rust owns the decision (pure `adaptive_batch_pages` + `BatchSizePolicy::from_env` + injected-reader `effective_pages`; no-dep total-RAM readers `sysctl -n hw.memsize` / `/proc/meminfo` `MemTotal`, pure parsers gated `#[cfg(any(target_os, test))]`) and sets the child's `SPECFORGE_INGEST_BATCH_PAGES` — the Docling helper is unchanged. `SPECFORGE_INGEST_ADAPTIVE_BATCH=off` forces the fixed ceiling; unreadable RAM keeps the ceiling. **Every `>= 16 GB` host (incl. the 24 GB dev host) lands on the 64 ceiling → re-ingests BYTE-IDENTICAL** (live-proven: adaptive-on vs `off` `diff -r` identical; ceiling 32 → 3 batches, same 72p/98 assets). **+7 tests, lib 1604 → 1611; full `run_ci.sh` GREEN + kg-bench.** Book "Sizing the batch to the host" + troubleshooting note; KM `ingest-adaptive-batch-sizing`. Runs at the FIRST statement of `materialize_pdf`, before any staging dir — so a refusal touches nothing and the prior bundle is trivially intact. A precise per-doc estimate is ill-posed pre-ingest (asset count unknown; page count only in the Docling subprocess), so the requirement scales off the cheap signal Rust has — the source PDF file size — `required_mb = 128 + source_mb × 4` (conservative; staged-swap is the backstop). No new dep: free disk via POSIX `df -P -k` (`available_disk_mb` + pure `parse_df_available_kb`), permissive when `df` is unreadable. New typed `AppError::IngestAbortedForDisk`; one knob `SPECFORGE_INGEST_MIN_FREE_DISK_MB` (estimate / fixed floor / off). 3 stub-helper ingest tests set it off; fixed a `collapsible_if` lint + a real PATH-spawn test race (spawning tests now hold `env_var_lock()`). **+8 tests, lib 1596 → 1604; full `run_ci.sh` GREEN + kg-bench 156/156.** Book "Pre-flight disk check" + troubleshooting entry; KM `ingest-disk-preflight`. - ✅ **`MEMORY-BOUNDED-INGEST.4a` (`2026-06-14`) — Done: built-in autonomous RAM guard — ingest aborts cleanly before it could crash the host.** The RAM guard that made the `.2`/`.3` big-PDF runs safe was an external shell wrapper; it is now first-class in `ingest`. While the Docling subprocess runs, `specforge` itself samples system memory (no new dep — `memory_pressure` free% on macOS, `/proc/meminfo` `MemAvailable` on Linux) and aborts cleanly before crossing a configurable danger ceiling. `materialize_pdf` swaps the blocking `.output()` for `run_backend_with_ram_guard`: pre-spawn sample (a heavy ingest never launches on an already-stressed host) → spawn with stdout/stderr → temp files → poll `try_wait` every 50 ms, sample memory every `sample_interval`, `kill()` on breach. Reader injected (`&dyn Fn() -> Option<f64>`) so abort/kill/complete are tested with **no real pressure**; pure platform parsers gated `#[cfg(any(target_os = "…", test))]` unit-test on both OSes. New typed `AppError::IngestAbortedForMemory`; on abort only `normalized.staging` is discarded — last-good `normalized/` + `source_ir.json` intact. Config `SPECFORGE_INGEST_RAM_ABORT_PERCENT` (85; off/out-of-range disables) / `SPECFORGE_INGEST_RAM_SAMPLE_SECS` (2, floor 1). 3 stub-helper ingest tests set the guard off for hot-CI determinism. **+9 tests, lib 1587 → 1596; full `run_ci.sh` GREEN + kg-bench 156/156.** Book "Autonomous host-memory safeguard" + troubleshooting entry; KM `ingest-ram-guard`. Fix: the Docling helper generates page images in memory but **skips persisting the per-page PNG** for large docs (`_env_flag` + `save_page_images` gate in `process_converted_document`) → ingest disk `O(assets)` not `O(pages)`; region crops full-res; `page_image_path` honest `None` with dimensions retained. Default skips above `SPECFORGE_INGEST_BATCH_THRESHOLD`, persists at/below (small docs byte-identical); override `SPECFORGE_INGEST_SAVE_PAGE_IMAGES=1/0`. **Verified A/B/C/D on a throwaway CAN copy + stash-rebuild byte-identity (new default == old default, bit-for-bit); region crops byte-identical save-vs-skip; 21 MB page PNGs vs 1.8 MB crops dropped.** No Rust struct change. Full `run_ci.sh` GREEN (1587). Book "Bounded disk footprint" subsection + KM `page-image-disk-bounding`. - ✅ **`MEMORY-BOUNDED-INGEST.1` (`2026-06-14`) — Done: bounded-memory ingestion of very large PDFs (owner-directed; surfaced by the CHI `.13c` OOM that twice memory-killed the 24 GB host).** Root cause: the Docling helper held a full-res image for every page at once (`convert(whole_pdf)` + `generate_page_images` @ `images_scale=2.0`) → peak RAM ∝ page count → CHI (585p) killed. Every doc we ingest OK is ≤500p; all failures are >500p (CHI 585/GIC 930/CoreSight 842/SMMU 717/Cortex-A76 620/USB 548). Fix: convert big PDFs in **bounded page ranges** (`convert(path, page_range=(lo,hi))`, Docling 2.84), persist+free each batch → peak O(batch). Extracted the two per-doc loops into a shared `process_converted_document` + `_IngestAccumulator`; `detect_pdf_page_count` (pypdfium2, cheap) gates the path. Batching only above `SPECFORGE_INGEST_BATCH_THRESHOLD` (default 512, above every current doc); batch size `SPECFORGE_INGEST_BATCH_PAGES` (default 64) — so all gold/intact docs (≤500p) keep the EXACT single-pass call. **Verified (temp 14p, no gold touched): (A) single-pass byte-identical before/after the refactor; (B) batched complete+correct — `page_no` absolute across batches, all structured surfaces match, only a benign boilerplate-header boundary split differs.** py_compile + full `run_ci.sh` GREEN (1587); RAM ~85% free throughout. Fix: the `else` arm now copies one whole UTF-8 char (`text[index..].chars().next()` → `push(ch)` → `+= ch.len_utf8()`); `index` is always on a char boundary, so pure-ASCII output is byte-for-byte identical to the old copy. +4 hermetic tests; lib 1583→**1587**; kg-bench **156/156**; full `run_ci.sh` GREEN. **Byte-stability re-proof: pre-fix vs post-fix fresh `evidence --dry-run` over the 15 non-ACE intact bundles = ALL BYTE-IDENTICAL** (ACE excluded — it OOMs pre-fix) → pure no-op on the intact corpus. **ACE evidence now COMPLETES: 21 s / 56 MB max RSS.** KM `prior-phrase-utf8-byte-as-char`; book troubleshooting note. - 🔗 DEMPSTER-FUSION-COMBINER (R16/R15e, `2026-06-04`) — **CLOSED** (user pick #2, after prior-decay): replaced the conservative `min` confidence in multimodal contract fusion (`ir/fusion.rs::merge_cluster`) with **Dempster's rule of combination** (Dempster 1967, `multimodal-fusion.md` gap). When independent sources **agree** on a contract, their confidences now *corroborate* instead of capping at the weakest: each ordinal confidence → belief mass (High .9 / Med .7 / Low .5), combined `m = 1 − ∏(1 − mᵢ)`, mapped back (≥.9 High, ≥.7 Medium). So **Medium+Medium → High** (`1−0.3·0.3=0.91`), Low+Low → Medium, High caps, a single source is unchanged. **Disagreement is untouched** — still routed to a Residual with the conservative `min` (conflict is never corroborated).
